new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5696389
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/ContourField.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,101 @@
+/**
+ * @license
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ */
+
+import React, { useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
+import { useI18n } from '../core/i18n';
+
+/** Closed, smooth contour around (cx,cy): radius wobbles with a few harmonics. */
+function contour(cx: number, cy: number, R: number, seed: number, n = 18): string {
+  const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+    const th = (2 * Math.PI * i) / n;
+    const w =
+      0.09 * Math.sin(2 * th + seed) +
+      0.06 * Math.sin(3 * th + seed * 1.7) +
+      0.035 * Math.sin(5 * th + seed * 2.3);
+    const r = R * (1 + w);
+    pts.push([cx + r * Math.cos(th), cy + r * 0.82 * Math.sin(th)]);
+  }
+  let d = `M${pts[0][0].toFixed(1)} ${pts[0][1].toFixed(1)}`;
+  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+    const p0 = pts[(i - 1 + n) % n], p1 = pts[i], p2 = pts[(i + 1) % n], p3 = pts[(i + 2) % n];
+    const c1 = [p1[0] + (p2[0] - p0[0]) / 6, p1[1] + (p2[1] - p0[1]) / 6];
+    const c2 = [p2[0] - (p3[0] - p1[0]) / 6, p2[1] - (p3[1] - p1[1]) / 6];
+    d += `C${c1[0].toFixed(1)} ${c1[1].toFixed(1)} ${c2[0].toFixed(1)} ${c2[1].toFixed(1)} ${p2[0].toFixed(1)} ${p2[1].toFixed(1)}`;
+  }
+  return d + 'Z';
+}
+
+const W = 520, H = 380;
+// Bounding box of the artwork = Senegal river valley → Atlas, purely decorative but plausible.
+const LON0 = -17.5, LON1 = -4.5, LAT0 = 12.5, LAT1 = 22.5;
+
+/**
+ * Decorative topographic field. Moving the pointer reads out coordinates
+ * (a survey-desk detail that also hints at what the tool is for).
+ */
+export const ContourField: React.FC<{ className?: string }> = ({ className = '' }) => {
+  const { t } = useI18n();
+  const ref = useRef<SVGSVGElement>(null);
+  const [cur, setCur] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
+
+  const paths = useMemo(() => {
+    const out: Array<{ d: string; major: boolean }> = [];
+    for (let k = 1; k <= 15; k++) out.push({ d: contour(185, 205, k * 13.5, 0.6 + k * 0.11), major: k % 5 === 0 });
+    for (let k = 1; k <= 8; k++) out.push({ d: contour(385, 140, k * 12.5, 2.4 + k * 0.14), major: k % 4 === 0 });
+    return out;
+  }, []);
+
+  const onMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
+    const r = ref.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
+    setCur({ x: ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * W, y: ((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height) * H });
+  };
+
+  const lon = cur ? LON0 + (cur.x / W) * (LON1 - LON0) : 0;
+  const lat = cur ? LAT1 - (cur.y / H) * (LAT1 - LAT0) : 0;
+  const fmt = (v: number, pos: string, neg: string) => `${Math.abs(v).toFixed(4)}° ${v >= 0 ? pos : neg}`;
+
+  return (
+    <svg
+      ref={ref}
+      viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`}
+      className={`w-full h-auto touch-none ${className}`}
+      onPointerMove={onMove}
+      onPointerLeave={() => setCur(null)}
+      role="img"
+      aria-label="Topographic contour illustration"
+    >
+      {/* graticule */}
+      <g stroke="var(--og-line)" strokeWidth="1">
+        {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((i) => <line key={`v${i}`} x1={(W / 6) * i} y1="0" x2={(W / 6) * i} y2={H} />)}
+        {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => <line key={`h${i}`} x1="0" y1={(H / 5) * i} x2={W} y2={(H / 5) * i} />)}
+      </g>
+      {/* contours */}
+      <g fill="none" strokeLinejoin="round">
+        {paths.map((p, i) => (
+          <path key={i} d={p.d} stroke={p.major ? 'var(--og-accent)' : 'var(--og-contour)'} strokeWidth={p.major ? 1.5 : 1} opacity={p.major ? 0.85 : 1} />
+        ))}
+      </g>
+      {/* summits */}
+      <g fill="var(--og-accent)">
+        <circle cx="190" cy="200" r="3.2" /><circle cx="390" cy="130" r="3.2" />
+      </g>
+      {/* crosshair + readout */}
+      {cur && (
+        <g pointerEvents="none">
+          <line x1={cur.x} y1="0" x2={cur.x} y2={H} stroke="var(--og-accent)" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="3 4" />
+          <line x1="0" y1={cur.y} x2={W} y2={cur.y} stroke="var(--og-accent)" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="3 4" />
+          <circle cx={cur.x} cy={cur.y} r="4.5" fill="none" stroke="var(--og-accent)" strokeWidth="1.5" />
+        </g>
+      )}
+      <g fontFamily="JetBrains Mono, monospace" fontSize="11">
+        <rect x="12" y={H - 34} width="226" height="22" rx="5" fill="var(--og-surface)" stroke="var(--og-line-strong)" />
+        <text x="22" y={H - 19} fill={cur ? 'var(--og-fg)' : 'var(--og-fg-3)'}>
+          {cur ? `${fmt(lat, 'N', 'S')}  ${fmt(lon, 'E', 'W')}` : t.shell.heroHint}
+        </text>
+      </g>
+    </svg>
+  );
+};

+++ b/src/components/SideRail.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,198 @@
+/**
+ * @license
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ */
+
+import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import {
+  Sparkles,
+  Layers,
+  Settings2,
+  Map as MapIcon,
+  Activity,
+  FileCode2,
+  BookOpen,
+  Check,
+  PanelLeftClose,
+  PanelLeftOpen,
+} from 'lucide-react';
+import { Logo } from './Logo';
+import { useI18n } from '../core/i18n';
+import type { NavTab } from './Header';
+
+export interface RailMeta {
+  datasetCount: number;
+  readyCount: number;
+  selectedName: string | null;
+  format: string;
+  crs: string;
+  running: number;
+  completed: number;
+  failed: number;
+}
+
+interface SideRailProps {
+  activeTab: NavTab;
+  setActiveTab: (tab: NavTab) => void;
+  meta: RailMeta;
+}
+
+const STORAGE_KEY = 'omnigeo_rail_collapsed';
+
+/**
+ * The pipeline rail. OmniGeo is a workflow (import → configure → inspect → run),
+ * so navigation shows where you are in it and what each stage currently holds,
+ * instead of a flat row of tabs.
+ */
+export const SideRail: React.FC<SideRailProps> = ({ activeTab, setActiveTab, meta }) => {
+  const { t } = useI18n();
+  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState<boolean>(() => {
+    try {
+      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
+      if (saved !== null) return saved === '1';
+    } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }
+    return typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.innerWidth < 1280;
+  });
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, collapsed ? '1' : '0'); } catch { /* ignore */ }
+  }, [collapsed]);
+
+  const s = t.shell;
+  const importMeta = meta.datasetCount > 0
+    ? `${meta.datasetCount} ${s.files} · ${meta.readyCount} ${s.ready}`
+    : s.dropToBegin;
+  const runMeta = meta.running > 0
+    ? `${meta.running} ${s.running}`
+    : meta.completed + meta.failed > 0
+      ? `${meta.completed} ${s.completed}${meta.failed ? ` · ${meta.failed} ${s.failed}` : ''}`
+      : s.idle;
+
+  const steps: Array<{
+    id: NavTab; n: string; label: string; sub: string; Icon: React.ElementType; done: boolean; live: boolean;
+  }> = [
+    { id: 'workspace', n: '01', label: t.header.workspaceTab, sub: importMeta, Icon: Layers, done: meta.datasetCount > 0, live: false },
+    { id: 'lab', n: '02', label: t.header.labTab, sub: `${meta.format.toUpperCase()} · ${meta.crs}`, Icon: Settings2, done: false, live: false },
+    { id: 'map', n: '03', label: t.header.mapTab, sub: meta.selectedName ?? s.noSelection, Icon: MapIcon, done: false, live: false },
+    { id: 'monitor', n: '04', label: t.header.monitorTab, sub: runMeta, Icon: Activity, done: meta.completed > 0 && meta.running === 0, live: meta.running > 0 },
+  ];
+
+  const toolkit: Array<{ id: NavTab; label: string; Icon: React.ElementType }> = [
+    { id: 'scripts', label: t.header.scriptsTab, Icon: FileCode2 },
+    { id: 'showcase', label: t.header.showcaseTab, Icon: BookOpen },
+  ];
+
+  const itemTitle = (label: string) => (collapsed ? label : undefined);
+
+  return (
+    <aside
+      aria-label="OmniGeo"
+      className={`hidden md:flex shrink-0 flex-col border-e border-[var(--og-line)] bg-[var(--og-surface)] transition-[width] duration-200 ${
+        collapsed ? 'w-[68px]' : 'w-[248px]'
+      }`}
+    >
+      {/* Brand */}
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        onClick={() => setActiveTab('craft')}
+        title="OmniGeo by Maodo"
+        className={`flex items-center gap-3 h-[60px] shrink-0 border-b border-[var(--og-line)] text-start ${collapsed ? 'justify-center px-0' : 'px-4'}`}
+      >
+        <Logo size={34} />
+        {!collapsed && (
+          <span className="leading-none min-w-0">
+            <span className="block font-display text-[17px] font-bold tracking-tight text-[var(--og-fg)]">OmniGeo</span>
+            <span className="block mt-1 og-eyebrow !text-[9px] !tracking-[0.1em] truncate">{t.header.brandSubtitle}</span>
+          </span>
+        )}
+      </button>
+
+      <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-4 space-y-5" aria-label="Primary">
+        {/* Studio */}
+        <button
+          type="button"
+          className="og-rail-item"
+          aria-current={activeTab === 'craft' ? 'page' : undefined}
+          onClick={() => setActiveTab('craft')}
+          title={itemTitle(t.header.craftTab)}
+        >
+          <span className="grid place-items-center w-[26px] h-[26px] flex-none">
+            <Sparkles className="w-[17px] h-[17px]" />
+          </span>
+          {!collapsed && <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold">{t.header.craftTab}</span>}
+        </button>
+
+        {/* Pipeline */}
+        <div>
+          {!collapsed && <div className="og-eyebrow px-2.5 mb-2">{s.pipeline}</div>}
+          <ol className="relative space-y-1">
+            {/* connecting survey line */}
+            <span
+              aria-hidden="true"
+              className="absolute top-4 bottom-4 w-px bg-[var(--og-line-strong)]"
+              style={{ insetInlineStart: collapsed ? 'calc(50% - 0.5px)' : '23px' }}
+            />
+            {steps.map(({ id, n, label, sub, Icon, done, live }) => (
+              <li key={id}>
+                <button
+                  type="button"
+                  className={`og-rail-item ${collapsed ? 'justify-center !px-0' : ''}`}
+                  aria-current={activeTab === id ? 'page' : undefined}
+                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(id)}
+                  title={itemTitle(`${n} · ${label}`)}
+                >
+                  <span className="og-node" data-done={done && activeTab !== id} data-live={live}>
+                    {done && activeTab !== id ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" strokeWidth={2.6} /> : n}
+                  </span>
+                  {!collapsed && (
+                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 leading-tight">
+                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-semibold">
+                        <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 opacity-70" />
+                        <span className="truncate">{label}</span>
+                      </span>
+                      <span className="block mt-0.5 truncate text-[11px] text-[var(--og-fg-3)] font-mono">{sub}</span>
+                    </span>
+                  )}
+                </button>
+              </li>
+            ))}
+          </ol>
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Toolkit */}
+        <div>
+          {!collapsed && <div className="og-eyebrow px-2.5 mb-2">{s.toolkit}</div>}
+          <ul className="space-y-1">
+            {toolkit.map(({ id, label, Icon }) => (
+              <li key={id}>
+                <button
+                  type="button"
+                  className={`og-rail-item ${collapsed ? 'justify-center !px-0' : ''}`}
+                  aria-current={activeTab === id ? 'page' : undefined}
+                  onClick={() => setActiveTab(id)}
+                  title={itemTitle(label)}
+                >
+                  <span className="grid place-items-center w-[26px] h-[26px] flex-none">
+                    <Icon className="w-[17px] h-[17px]" />
+                  </span>
+                  {!collapsed && <span className="text-[13px] font-medium truncate">{label}</span>}
+                </button>
+              </li>
+            ))}
+          </ul>
+        </div>
+      </nav>
+
+      <button
+        type="button"
+        onClick={() => setCollapsed((c) => !c)}
+        aria-label={collapsed ? s.expand : s.collapse}
+        title={collapsed ? s.expand : s.collapse}
+        className={`flex items-center gap-2 h-11 shrink-0 border-t border-[var(--og-line)] text-[12px] text-[var(--og-fg-3)] hover:text-[var(--og-fg)] transition-colors ${collapsed ? 'justify-center' : 'px-5'}`}
+      >
+        {collapsed ? <PanelLeftOpen className="w-4 h-4 rtl:-scale-x-100" /> : <PanelLeftClose className="w-4 h-4 rtl:-scale-x-100" />}
+        {!collapsed && <span>{s.collapse}</span>}
+      </button>
+    </aside>
+  );

+++ b/src/components/StatusBar.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,64 @@
+/**
+ * @license
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ */
+
+import React from 'react';
+import { WifiOff, FlaskConical } from 'lucide-react';
+import { useI18n } from '../core/i18n';
+import type { RailMeta } from './SideRail';
+
+interface StatusBarProps {
+  meta: RailMeta;
+  crsMode: 'reproject' | 'assign';
+  isDryRun: boolean;
+  workers: number;
+}
+
+/** Always-visible run context, like the footer of a desktop GIS. */
+export const StatusBar: React.FC<StatusBarProps> = ({ meta, crsMode, isDryRun, workers }) => {
+  const { t } = useI18n();
+  const s = t.shell;
+  const Sep = () => <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-3 w-px bg-[var(--og-line-strong)]" />;
+
+  return (
+    <footer
+      role="contentinfo"
+      className="h-7 shrink-0 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 border-t border-[var(--og-line)] bg-[var(--og-surface)] text-[11px] font-mono text-[var(--og-fg-3)] select-none overflow-hidden whitespace-nowrap"
+    >
+      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--og-ok)]">
+        <WifiOff className="w-3 h-3" />
+        <span className="hidden sm:inline">{s.offlineReady}</span>
+      </span>
+      <Sep />
+      <span>
+        <b className="font-semibold text-[var(--og-fg-2)]">{meta.datasetCount}</b> {s.files}
+        {meta.datasetCount > 0 && <> · <b className="font-semibold text-[var(--og-fg-2)]">{meta.readyCount}</b> {s.ready}</>}
+      </span>
+      <Sep />
+      <span className="hidden md:inline">
+        {meta.format.toUpperCase()} <span className="text-[var(--og-fg-3)]">→</span>{' '}
+        <b className="font-semibold text-[var(--og-accent)]">{meta.crs}</b>
+      </span>
+      <span className="hidden lg:inline-flex items-center gap-3">
+        <Sep />
+        <span>{s.mode}: <span className="text-[var(--og-fg-2)]">{crsMode === 'reproject' ? s.reproject : s.assign}</span></span>
+      </span>
+      {isDryRun && (
+        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-1.5 py-px bg-[var(--og-accent-soft)] text-[var(--og-accent)]">
+          <FlaskConical className="w-3 h-3" /> {s.dryRun}
+        </span>
+      )}
+      <span className="ms-auto flex items-center gap-3">
+        {meta.running > 0 && (
+          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[var(--og-accent)]">
+            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[var(--og-accent)] animate-pulse" />
+            {meta.running} {s.running}
+          </span>
+        )}
+        <span className="hidden md:inline">{workers} {s.workers}</span>
+        <span className="hidden md:inline"><kbd className="og-kbd">{t.header.commandsKbd}</kbd></span>
+      </span>
+    </footer>
+  );
+};
